## Shared source texts

### T0409e1b17b8039a36b5481ef

Text hash fb956c0a310b1a6dbd1b88328e6e100e5808eff8d74115458da4f6cddd3f892d; absolute characters 1970–2391; files: jobs/app-executive-orders/output/texts/executive-order-10875-creating-emergency-board-investigate-disputes-between-the-akron.txt

```text
As provided by section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, as amended, from this date and for thirty days after the board has made its report to the President, no change, except by agreement, shall be made by the Akron & Barberton Belt Railroad or any other carrier represented by the Eastern, Western, and Southeastern Carriers' Conference Committees, or by their employees, in the conditions out of which these disputes arose.
```

### T06e2c2126c690d286208969d

Text hash 2835140dd3e6b81a7c584ac26136a0bb3d78bcf7c2fc5775da5039d53850d4c0; absolute characters 965–1098; files: jobs/app-executive-orders/output/texts/executive-order-10165-creating-emergency-board-investigate-dispute-between-the-railway.txt

```text
The board shall report its findings to the President with respect to the said dispute within thirty days from the date of this order.
```

### T087aa1423ebc4aa5a836ef19

Text hash fb956c0a310b1a6dbd1b88328e6e100e5808eff8d74115458da4f6cddd3f892d; absolute characters 1839–1968; files: jobs/app-executive-orders/output/texts/executive-order-10875-creating-emergency-board-investigate-disputes-between-the-akron.txt

```text
The board shall report its findings to the President with respect to the disputes within thirty days from the date of this order.

Executive Order 10876
AMENDMENT OF EXECUTIVE ORDER  
NO. 10855,1  AUTHORIZING THE  
INSPECTION OF CERTAIN TAX RE­
TURNS
By virtue of the authority vested in me 
by sections 55(a) and 508 of the Internal 
Revenue Code of 1939 (53 Stat. 29, 111, 
54 Stat. 1008; 26 U.S.C. 55(a) and 508), 
and by section 6103(a) of the Internal 
Revenue Code of 1954 (68A Stat. 753; 26 
U.S.C. 6103(a)), it is hereby ordered 
that the first paragraph of Executive 
Order No. 10855 of November 27, 1959, 
entitled “Inspection of Income Tax Re­
turns by the Senate Committee on the 
Judiciary”, be, and it is hereby, amended
(1) by deleting therefrom “ 1945 to 1958” 
and inserting in lieu thereof “ 1945 to 
1959”, and (2) by inserting therein “or 
Senate Resolution 238, 86th Congress, 
agreed to February 8,1960,” immediately 
after “Senate Resolution 57, 86th Con­
gress, agreed to February 2, 1959,”.
This order shall be effective upon its 
filing for publication in the F ederal  
R egister .
D w ig h t D. E is e n h o w e r
T h e W h it e H o u s e,
April 22,1960.
[F.R. Doc. 60-3813; Filed, Apr. 25, 1960;
9:50 a.m.]

### T16b56e64657763ccc8c7aefc

Text hash 006a08cade6e1ea0bfbbd68c51ca665a8d4718ff941e7523aa78b278cd5ecba4; absolute characters 4674–4942; files: jobs/source-ladder/output/texts/queue_EO_10509_Actual_report_date_of_the_board_created_by_EO_10509_Presidential_Emergency_Board_Rail_e2f909c93f.txt

```text
Inasmuch as the 30-day period for completion of the hearings and 
the filing of this report would have expired on January 15, 1954, the 
parties joined in a stipulation extending the time to and including 
February 17, 1954, and the President approved the stipulation.
```

### T16fab49d56e13be5b5fc510c

Text hash 99ccb1946c725fd1712abbc9a1d38955ca02d0d0fee7ef85a2c05ce8da09894c; absolute characters 11060–11312; files: jobs/source-ladder/output/texts/queue_EO_10165_Actual_report_date_of_the_board_created_by_EO_10165_Presidential_Emergency_Board_Rail_73f9bab795.txt

```text
As-instances, we note the depression wage cut in the general trucking 
industry was restored in the New York area September 1,1934, while 
the express drivers did not get theirs back until April 1,1935, the date 
that all railroad employees got theirs.
```

### T1ae500224d7a584bc2e1553b

Text hash de81e37036ce6a97b9901d3869846f6195a04d3ed220349fd72306466db4c62c; absolute characters 5279–5523; files: jobs/source-ladder/output/texts/queue_EO_10511_Actual_report_date_of_the_board_created_by_EO_10511_Presidential_Emergency_Board_Rail_7d5b92513c.txt

```text
The original date for the submission of the Board's report and 
recommendations was extended from time to time and finally, by 
agreement between the parties with the approval of the National 
Mediation Board and the President~ to May 15, 1954.

Presidential Documents
Title 3— THE PRESIDENT
Executive Order 10888
CREATING AN EMERGENCY BOARD  
TO INVESTIGATE A DISPUTE BE­
TWEEN CERTAIN CARRIERS REP­
RESENTED BY THE NEW YORK  
HARBOR CARRIERS’ CONFERENCE  
COMMITTEE A N D  CERTAIN OF  
THEIR EMPLOYEES
WHEREAS a dispute exists between 
certain carriers represented by the New 
York Harbor Carriers’  Conference Com­
mittee, designated in List A attached 
hereto and hereby made a part hereof, 
and certain of their employees repre­
sented by labor organizations, members 
of the Railroad Marine Harbor Council, 
AFL-CIO, designated in List B attached 
hereto and hereby made a part hereof; 
and
WHEREAS this dispute has not here­
tofore been adjusted under the provisions 
of the Railway Labor Act, as amended; 
and
WHEREAS this dispute, in the judg­
ment of the National Mediation Board, 
threatens substantially to interrupt in­
terstate commerce to a degree such as to 
deprive a section of the country of 
essential transportation service:
NOW, THEREFORE, by virtue of the 
authority vested in me by section 10 of 
the Railway Labor Act, as amended 
(45 U.S.C. 160), I hereby create a board 
of three members, to be appointed by me, 
to investigate this dispute. No member 
of the board shall be pecuniarily or other­
wise interested in any organization of 
railroad employees or any carrier.
The board shall report its findings to 
the President with respect to the dispute 
within thirty days from the date of this 
order.
As provided by section 10 of the Rail­
way Labor Act, as amended, from this 
date and for thirty days after the board 
has made its report to the President, no 
change, except by agreement, shall be 
made by carriers represented by the New 
York Harbor Carriers’  Conference Com­
mittee, or by their employees, in the con­
ditions out of which the dispute arose.
D wight  D. E isenhower
T he  W hite  H ouse ,
September 28, 1960.

Pilots

PART 68— R E G U L A T IO N S  AND 
STANDARDS FOR INSPECTION AND  
CERTIFICATION OF CERTAIN AGRI­
CULTURAL COMMODITIES A N D  
PRODUCTS THEREOF
Loaning of Samples
Pursuant to the Agricultural Market­
ing Act of 1946, as amended (7 U.S.C. 
1621 et seq.), the regulations for the in­
spection and certification of certain agri­
cultural commodities and products 
thereof (7 CFR Part 68) are hereby 
amended by adding the following 
section:
§ 6 8 .5 4  L oaning o f  sam ples.
In the discretion of the Director, 
limited numbers of samples illustrating 
the United States standards under the 
Acts, or specially prepared exhibits illus­
trating any part of such standards, may 
be loaned by the Department without 
charge to governmental agencies for 
official purposes or to educational institu­
tions of higher learning and to trade or­
ganizations for demonstration purposes.
(Secs. 203, 205, 60 Stat. 1087, 1090, as  
amended; 7 U.S.C. 1622,1624)

tion Service (Farm Marketing Quo­
tas and Acreage Allotments),  
Department of Agriculture
[Arndt. 2]
PART 728— WHEAT
Subpart— Determination of County  
Normal Yields for 1960  
N ebraska; C herry C ounty
Basis and purpose.  The amendment 
herein is issued under and in accordance 
with the provisions of the Agricultural 
Adjustment Act of 1938, as amended, 
to correct an error in this subpart pub­
lished in the F ederal R egister (25 F.R. 
6116).
Since the determination of 1960 county 
normal yields is necessary for use as a 
guide in the determination of 1960 nor­
mal yields for excess farms, it is neces­
sary to make this correction immediately. 
Therefore, it is hereby found and de­
termined that compliance with the pub­
lic notice, procedure, and effective date 
provisions of the Administrative Pro­
cedure Act (5 U.S.C. 1003) is unneces­
sary, and that this amendment shall 
become effective upon the date of its 
publication in the F ederal R egister. 
Section 728.1008 Determination of the  
county normal yields for the 1960 crop  
of wheat  is amended as follows:
For the State of Nebraska, as to Cherry 
County strike out the figures “26.1” and 
insert in lieu thereof the figures “19.2”. 
(Sec. 375, 52 Stat. 66; 7 U.S.C. 1375)
Issued at Washington, D.C., this 23d 
day of September, 1960.
F orest W. B eall, 
Acting Administrator,  
Commodity Stabilization Service.

WHEREAS it is the policy of this Gov­
ernment to foster mutual understand-

[the world's resources and higher stand­
ards of living; and
WHEREAS expanded world trade ad­
vances the ideal of unity among all man­
kind and strengthens the foundation for 
[lasting peace and prosperity:
N O W , THEREFORE, I, DWIGHT D. 
EISENHOWER, President of the United

WHEREAS a  dispute exists between 
the New York, Chicago &  St. Louis Rail­
road Company, a carrier, and certain of 
its employees represented by the Broth­
erhood of Railroad Trainmen, a labor 
organization; and
WHEREAS this dispute has not here­
tofore been adjusted under the provisions 
of the Railway Labor Act, as amended; 
and
WHEREAS this dispute, in the judg­
ment of the National Mediation Board, 
threatens substantially to interrupt in­
terstate commerce to a degree such as 
to deprive a section of the country of 
essential transportation service:
NOW, THEREFORE, by virtue of the 
authority vested in me by section 10 of 
the Railway Labor Act, as amended (45 
U. S. C. 160), I hereby create a board of 
three members, to be .  appointed by me, 
to investigate the said dispute. No 
member of the said board shall be 
pecuniarily or otherwise interested in 
any organization of employees or any 
carrier.
The board shali1  report its findings to 
the President with respect to the said 
dispute within thirty days from the date 
of this order.
As provided by section 10 of the Rail­
way Labor Act, as amended, from this 
date and for thirty days after the board 
has made its report to the President, no 
change, except by agreement, shall be 
made by the New York, Chicago &  St.

INTRODUCTION 
The President appointed Emergency Board No. 113 under Executive 
Order No. 10635, dated September 1, 1955, pursuant to section 10 of 
the Railway Labor Act, as amended, to investigate and report on 
certain unadjusted disputes between the Pennsylvania Railroad Co. 
and certain of its employees represented by the Transport Workers 
Union of America, C.
```

should not be required to depart from the railroad pattern of across-

fil STER
VOLUME 15 1 9 3 4  c < yV N n t O ^  NUMBER 194
Washington, Friday, October 6, 1950
TITLE 3— THE PRESIDENT
EXECUTIVE ORDER 10165
Creating an E mergency B oard to I nves­
tigate a D ispute  B etween the R ail­
way Express Agency, I nc., and Certain 
op its E mployees
WHEREAS a dispute exists between 
the Railway Express Agency, Inc., a car­
rier, and certain of its employees repre­
sented by the International Brotherhood 
of Teamsters, Chauffeurs, Warehouse­
men &  Helpers of America, ft. F. of L., 
Locals 808 and 459, a labor organization; 
and
WHEREAS this dispute has not here­
tofore been adjusted under the provisions 
of the Railway Labor Act, as amended; 
and
WHEREAS this dispute, in the judg­
ment of the National Mediation Board, 
threatens substantially to interrupt in­
terstate commerce to a degree such as to 
deprive a section of the country of essen­
tial transportation service:
NOW, THEREFORE, by virtue of the 
authority vested in me by section 10 of 
the Railway Labor Act, as amended (45 
U. S. C. 160), I hereby create a board of 
three members, to be appointed by me, 
to investigate the said dispute. No mem­
ber of the said board shall be pecuniarily 
or otherwise interested in any organiza­
tion of railway employees or any carrier.
The board shall report its findings to 
the President with respect to the said 
dispute within thirty days from the date 
of this order.
As provided by section 10 of the Rail­
way Labor Act, as amended, from this 
date and for thirty days after the board 
has made its report to the President, no 
change, except by agreement, shall be 
made by the Railway Express Agency, 
Inc., or by its employees, in the condi­
tions out of which the said dispute arose.
Harry S. T ruman
The W hite  H ouse,
October 3, 1950.
[F. R. D O C . 50-8805; Filed, Oct. 4, 1950;
3:02 p. m.]

MISCELLANEOUS AMENDMENTS
The above-identified regulations (14 
F. R. 5290, 6674,15 F. R. 4161) are hereby 
amended for the 1951 and succeeding 
crop years as follows:
1. Section 416.9 (c) is amended to 
read as follows :
§  416.9 Death, incompetence, or dis­
appearance of insured. *  *  *
(c) If an applicant for insurance or 
an insured dies or is judicially declared 
incompetent less than 15 days before the 
closing date for the filing of applications 
for insurance in any year and before the 
beginning of planting of the corn crop 
intended to be covered by insurance, 
whoever succeeds him on the farm with 
the right to plant the corn crop as his 
heir or heirs, administrator, executor, 
guardian, committee or conservator, may 
be substituted for the applicant or the in­
sured upon filing with the county office, 
within 15 days (unless such period is ex­
tended in writing by the Corporation) 
after the date of such death or judicial 
declaration, a statement in writing in 
the form and manner prescribed by the 
Corporation, requesting such substitu­
tion and agreeing to assume the obliga­
tions of the original applicant with 
respect to such corn crop arising out of 
such application or the Contract: Pro­
vided, That any substitution made pur­
suant to this paragraph shall be effective 
only with respect to the corn crop to be 
planted in the ensuing crop year, and the 
contract shall terminate at the end of 
such year. If no such statement is ap­
proved by the Corporation, the applica­
tion shall be void or the contract shall 
terminate.
2. The following section is hereby 
added:
§  416.18 Monetary coverage for silage  
corn. Insurance on a monetary cover­
age basis shall include true type silage

Presidential Documents
Title 3— THE PRESIDENT
Executive Order 10862
CREATING AN EMERGENCY BOARD  
TO INVESTIGATE A DISPUTE BE­
TWEEN THE ATCHISON, TOPEKA  
AND SANTA FE RAILWAY COM­
PANY— COAST LINES AND CER­
TAIN OF ITS EMPLOYEES
WHEREAS a dispute exists between  
the Atchison, Topeka and Santa Fe Rail­
way Company— Coast Lines, a carrier,  
and certain of its employees represented 
by the Brotherhood of Locomotive En­
gineers, a labor organization; and
WHEREAS this dispute has not here­
tofore been adjusted under the provi­
sions of the Railway Labor Act, as  
amended; and
WHEREAS this dispute, in the judg­
ment of the National Mediation Board,  
threatens substantially to interrupt in­
terstate commerce to a degree such as to  
deprive a section of the country of es­
sential transportation service:
NOW, THEREFORE, by virtue of the  
authority vested in me by section 10 of  
the Railway Labor Act, as amended (45  
U.S.C. 160), I hereby create a board of  
three members, to be appointed by me,  
to investigate this dispute. No member  
of the board shall be pecuniarily or  
otherwise interested in any organization  
of railway employees or any carrier.
The board shall report its findings to  
the President with respect to the dispute  
within thirty days from the date of this  
order.
As provided by section 10 of the Rail­
way Labor Act, as amended, from this  
date and for thirty days after the board  
has made its report to the President, no  
change, except by agreement, shall be  
made by the Atchison, Topeka and Santa  
Fe Railway Company— Coast Lines, or by 
its employees, in the conditions out of  
which the dispute arose.
D w ig h t D . E is e n h o w e r
T h e W h it e H o u s e,
February 12, 1960.
[F.R. Doc. 60-1520; Filed, Feb. 12, 1960;
5:08 p.m.]
rx

FEDERALREGISTER
VOLUME 20 1934* ^A/iteo  ^
Washington, Friday, September 2, 1955
NUMBER 172
TITLE 3— THE PRESIDENT
PROCLAMATION 3111
T e r m in a t io n of  E cuadoran  T rade  A gree ­
m e n t P roclam ation
BY THE PRESIDENT OF THE UNITED STATES  
OF AMERICA 
A PROCLAMATION
WHEREAS, under the authority 
vested in him by section 350 (a) of the 
Tariff Act of 1930, as amended by the 
act of June 12, 1934, entitled “An Act 
to amend the Tariff Act of 1930“ (48 
Stat. 943), the time within which the 
President was authorized to enter into 
trade agreements pursuant to such 
amending act having been extended for 
three years from June 12, 1937, by the 
joint resolution of Congress approved 
March 1, 1937 (50 Stat. 24), the Presi­
dent of the United States entered into 
a trade agreement with the Supreme 
Chief of the Republic of Ecuador on 
August 6, 1938 (53 Stat. 1952), and pro­
claimed such trade agreement by proc­
lamation of September 23,1938 (53 Stat. 
1951); and
WHEREAS Article X IX  of the said 
trade agreement provides that the agree­
ment shall remain in force and effect 
until six months from the day on which 
either Government shall give notice of 
its intention to terminate it; and 
WHEREAS, pursuant to the said Ar­
ticle X IX  the Government of the United 
States of America gave notice on July 
18, 1955, of its intention to terminate 
the said trade agreement; and 
WHEREAS the said section 350 (a) 
of the Tariff Act of 1930, as amended, 
authorizes the President to terminate, 
in whole or in part, any proclamation 
carrying out a trade agreement entered 
into under such section:
NOW, THEREFORE, I, DWIGHT D. 
EISENHOWER, President of the United 
States of America, acting under and by 
virtue of the authority vested in me by 
the Constitution and the statutes, in­
cluding the said section 350 (a) of the 
Tariff Act of 1930, as amended, do 
proclaim that the said proclamation 
dated September 23, 1938, shall be 
terminated as of the close of January 17, 
1956, six months from the day on which 
notice of termination of the said trade 
agreement was given by the Government 
of the United States of America.
IN  WITNESS WHEREOF, I  have here­
unto set my hand and caused the Seal of 
the United States of America to be 
affixed.
DONE at the City of Washington this 
twenty-seventh day of August in the year 
of our Lord nineteen hundred 
[ se al ] and fifty-five, and of the Inde­
pendence of the United States of 
America the one hundred and eightieth.
D w ig h t D. E ise n h o w e r 
By the President:
Jo h n F oster  D u l l e s,
Secretary of State.
[P. R. Doc. 55-7155; Piled, Aug. 31, 1955;  
2:02 p. m.j
EXECUTIVE ORDER 10635
C reating  a n E m ergency  B oard  to  I n v e s­
tigate  a D isp u t e B e t w e e n th e P e n n­
s ylv a n ia R ailroad  and  C ertain  of  I ts 
E m plo yees
WHEREAS a dispute exists between 
the Pennsylvania Railroad, a carrier, 
and certain of its employees repre­
sented by the Transport Workers Union 
of America, C. I. O., Railroad Division, 
a labor organization; and 
WHEREAS this dispute has not here­
tofore been adjusted under the provi­
sions of the Railway Labor Act, as 
amended; and
WHEREAS this dispute, in the judg­
ment of the National Mediation Board, 
threatens substantially to interrupt in­
terstate commerce to a degree such as 
to deprive a section of the country of 
essential transportation service:
NOW, THEREFORE, by virtue of the 
authority vested in me by section 10 of 
the Railway Labor Act, as amended (45 
U. S. C. 160), I hereby create a board 
of three members, to be appointed by me, 
to investigate the said dispute. No mem­
ber of the said Board shall be pecuniarily 
or otherwise interested in any organiza­
tion of railway employees or any carrier.
The board shall report its findings to 
the President with respect to the said 
dispute within thirty days from the date 
of this order.
As provided by section 10 of the Rail­
way Labor Act, as amended, from this 
date and for thirty days after the board

Washington, Friday, January 1, 1954
TITLE 3— THE PRESIDENT
EXECUTIVE ORDER 10511
Creating an E mergency  B oard To I n­
vestigate D isputes  B etween  the 
Akron , C anton and Y oungstown  
R ailroad C ompany  and O ther C ar­
riers  and Certain of T heir  E mployees
WHEREAS disputes exist between the 
Akron, Canton and Youngstown Railroad 
Company and other carriers represented 
by the Eastern, Western andrSoutheast- 
ern Carriers’  Conference Committees, 
designated in list A attached hereto and 
made a part hereof, and certain of their 
employees represented by the fifteen co­
operating (non-operating) railway labor 
organizations designated in list B a t­
tached hereto and made a part hereof; 
and
WHEREAS these disputes have not 
heretofore been adjusted under the pro­
visions of the Railway Labor Act, as 
amended! and
WHEREAS these disputes, in the judg­
ment of the National Mediation Board, 
threaten substantially to interrupt in­
terstate commerce to a degree such as 
to deprive the country of essential 
transportation service:
NOW, THEREFORE, by virtue of the 
authority vested in me by section 10 of 
the Railway Labor Act, as amended (45 
U. S. C. 160), I hereby create a board 
of three members, to be appointed by 
me, to investigate the said disputes. 
No member of the said board shall be 
pecuniarily or otherwise interested in 
any organization of employees or any 
carrier.
The board shall report its findings to 
the President with respect to the said 
dispute within thirty days from the date 
of this order.
As provided by section 10 of the Rail­
way Labor Act, as amended, from this 
date and for thirty days after the board 
has made its report to the President, no 
change, except by agreement, shall be 
made by the Akron, Canton and Youngs­
town Railroad Company and other 
carriers represented by the Eastern, 
Western and Southeastern Carriers’  
Conference Committees or their em­
ployees in the conditions out of which 
the said disputes arose.
Dwight  D. E isenhower

,{"file_sha256":"c8cc95b7f444db26c21c8870802b845e795b2ec6a3763dc32b2ce354d7b978cf","path":"research/readiness/claims/claude-2026-10-31-apollo-g04.jsonl","pointer":"line:11"}],"reported_material_gaps":[],"research_rationale":"Identity: the registered original is the Federal Register printing of Executive Order 10165 (15 FR 6737; govinfo PDF text layer of the official page, grade original_representation_unverified), headed Executive Order 10165 and signed October 3, 1950. The order creates a three-member emergency board under section 10 of the Railway Labor Act to investigate the dispute between the Railway Express Agency, Inc. and certain of its employees represented by the International Brotherhood of Teamsters, Locals 808 and 459, requires the board to report within thirty days from the date of the order. Inventory: the order is one operative unit; the creation of the board, its thirty-day report deadline and the status-quo sentence restating section 10 are all within the single whole-order clause confirmed here. Ending: Executive Order 12553 of February 25, 1986 (51 FR 7237, held government printing) revokes the numbered orders in its table, and any amendments thereto; its No. Subject table lists 10165 with a subject line matching this order (creating an emergency board to investigate a railroad labor dispute). The enacting provision has no stated effective date, so the revocation is read as effective on signing, 1986-02-25; the Federal Register publication date 1986-03-03 and the filing date are not effective dates. The thirty-day or stated report date and the end of any statutory status-quo period are deadlines and spent facts, not ends (owner ruling 2026-10-21 on report deadlines): the order stayed on the books until express revocation. Searches: the OFR executive-order catalog search and the USC and eCFR citation searches (coverage notes read; no coverage warning) show no instrument other than EO 12553 revoking, superseding or amending this order; the held-text citation search found citations of this number only in printings of the order itself, orders printed on the same page, Federal Register index or codification-guide material and (for some) National Mediation Board report front matter.\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Candidate earlier ends examined. (1) The thirty-day report deadline is a deadline, not an end (owner ruling 2026-10-21): the order does not end when the board reports, nor when the statutory thirty-day status-quo period after the report runs out; actual report and standstill dates are spent facts that no reviewed event record in this packet supplies, so they are disclosed here rather than recorded as later events. (2) The statutory standstill in section 10 of the Railway Labor Act is the statute's own effect and not a separate command of this order. (3) No later order amends, supersedes or revokes the order before EO 12553 in the held corpus (OFR catalog and number-citation searches). EO 12553 is the first held instrument that revokes it. No earlier end is shown, so none is claimed.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"Independent commands: the order has no command independent of the board's creation and reporting. The thirty-day report requirement and the status-quo sentence are parts of the same board-creating order and share its end; the status-quo sentence restates section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, whose force comes from that statute.\",\"savings_check\":\"Savings and residual effects: EO 12553's enacting provision contains no savings or exception clause (the ACT record for this pair lists none, and the held text has none). The board's work, report and any agreement reached are completed past events rather than continuing commands of this order; the order created no right, appointment or transfer that survives independently of it, and no held source shows any continuing independent effect.\",\"scope_check\":\"Scope: EO 12553 revokes the order by number and the held table row matches its subject; the enacting provision reaches any amendments thereto and has no exception, so it reaches the whole order, not part. Inventory corrected: two clauses, offsets 3394 to 4127 and 11203 to 11300 of the held text. The text layer interleaves the order with unrelated Federal Register page text (a contents list) between its first and second parts, so the order's operative text is the first part (the enacting words) and the continuation, which are contiguous in the printed order. The dossier's span includes the interleaved unrelated page text. The order's heading and recitals precede the first part and contain no command. Text: The order recites that a dispute exists between the New York, Chicago & St. Louis Railroad Company, a carrier, and certain of its employees represented by the Brotherhood of Railroad Trainmen, a labor organization. Acting under section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, as amended (45 U.S.C. 160), it creates a board of three members, to be appointed by the President, to investigate the dispute; it bars any board member from being pecuniarily or otherwise interested in any organization of employees or any carrier; it directs the board to report its findings to the President within thirty days from the date of the order; and, as provided by section 10, it bars the New York, Chicago & St. Louis Railroad Company or its employees from changing, except by agreement, the conditions out of which the dispute arose, from the date of the order until thirty days after the board has made its report to the President. Ending: Executive Order 12553 of February 25, 1986 (51 FR 7237, held govinfo text layer) has an enacting sentence reading, in part, \"it is hereby ordered that the following Executive Orders, and\" and, on the next line, \"any amendments thereto, are revoked:\" (the number 10449 appears in the table's number column, and the aligned subject entry in the separately extracted subject column (matched by position) reads as creating an emergency board to investigate a railroad labor dispute). The enacting sentence has no effective-date wording and no exception or saving clause, so the first end is dated on signing, 1986-02-25 (a reading disclosed for the reviewer; the March 3, 1986 printing date is not used). Report: the held National Mediation Board record (jobs/source-ladder/output/texts/queue_EO_10449_Actual_report_date_of_the_board_created_by_EO_10449_Presidential_Emergency_Board_Rail_890e74e86f.txt, a held text lead and not an authenticated original) has a National Mediation Board record for Emergency Board No. 104 stating that no members were appointed and that the parties reached agreement on April 26, 1953, so that no emergency board was set up for hearing the dispute; there is no report. Under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 on EO 10449 (a board never constituted is treated like the other emergency-board orders) the order stayed on the books until its express revocation; the fact that the board was never constituted is disclosed here, and the agreement of the parties on April 26, 1953 (a statement in that record) is a spent fact and not an end. The thirty-day report deadline (to May 24, 1953 on the inclusive reading) is a deadline and not an end. Prior research: the packet reports no prior claim or review for this order. Searches: current-law:usc, current-law:ecfr and later-events:ofr_catalog, each marked complete in the packet with no coverage warning; they are the packet's machine searches.\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Earlier-end check: the order's own terms contain a thirty-day report deadline and a standstill running until thirty days after the report, neither of which is an end; no stated fixed end date or termination clause appears in the held text. The packet's bounded number search of 33,118 held texts (clause drafts excluded) found citations only in this order's own printing. The dossier's chain of later events names only Executive Order 12553 as acting on this order, and the three selected machine searches show no revoking, superseding or terminating instrument before 1986-02-25.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"Independent commands: the creation of the board, the bar on interested members, the report deadline, the standstill all concern the single dispute the order addressed; none is a separate delegation, standing rule or continuing regulation. The standstill sentence restates section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, which continues on its own statutory force; the period it measures here was set by this order's creation of the board and nothing held shows it still running.\",\"savings_check\":\"Savings: Executive Order 12553 contains no saving clause (the enacting sentence is followed only by the table). 10509 is a single-paragraph Railway Labor Act section 10 emergency board order. Its only commands are creation of the board, a thirty-day report deadline and a recited statutory standstill. Under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 (report deadline, option C) none of those ends the order; the first held end is the express revocation by EO 12553 on 1986-02-25 (signature date, no effective-date wording). The board's report date 1954-02-17 is a spent fact recorded in the clause reason. Savings: none found in EO 12553 or in the order itself. Searches: U.S. Code and eCFR number-citation searches found no hit for this order; the OFR disposition-note search lists only the EO 12553 revocation in the chain.\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Checked for an earlier end: (1) the order's own terms contain a report deadline and a post-report standstill but no sunset, so the report and standstill dates are spent facts and not ends under the report-deadline ruling; (2) no extension, amendment or revocation of EO 10509 other than EO 12553 appears in the dossier chain or in the number-citation scan of held texts; the report-time extensions described in the held report (where a report is held) were stipulations approved by the President, not held instruments, and affect only the deadline; (3) a general later rule is not treated as an end, only EO 12553, which names the order by number.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"The standstill is recited as provided by section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, so any no-change duty it describes arises under that statute and not only from the order; by the order's own terms it ran only to thirty days after the board's report, which was long before 1986. The order creates no continuing right, office or appropriation. No other command in the order survives the board's report.\",\"savings_check\":\"The text of EO 12553 contains no savings, exception, effective-date or notwithstanding wording (the held text was searched for those terms). The board's investigation, report and the standstill were spent in the 1950s, and the order created no accrued right or later-effective duty. The statute the order invokes is not affected by revoking the order.\",\"scope_check\":\"One clause covers the whole order; the order has no partial-scope command, no exception and no delayed effective date. The revocation in EO 12553 has no exception text, so it reaches the whole order.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[{"file_sha256":"e9c80d0c381d5045f956cd6a0abb24f9683079df4265dbb4c01a66e1e97cdc1a","path":"research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g05/packet/complete-v1/receipts/research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g05/packet/reviews/BLIND_REVIEW.jsonl","pointer":"line:5","review_kind":"independent_ai_source_review","scope":"whole_case","verdict":"upheld","version_binding":"exact"},{"file_sha256":"e9c80d0c381d5045f956cd6a0abb24f9683079df4265dbb4c01a66e1e97cdc1a","path":"research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g05/packet/reviews/BLIND_REVIEW.jsonl","pointer":"line:5","review_kind":"independent_ai_source_review","scope":"whole_case","verdict":"upheld","version_binding":"exact"}],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:10509#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:10509#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"2d72b7098612c1a5d1dce60b40a9153703962d3fd4c1314fdcbb0c57e3599abb","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10509.txt","sha256":"2d72b7098612c1a5d1dce60b40a9153703962d3fd4c1314fdcbb0c57e3599abb","state":"match"},"sha256":"beb73967e0a9bb839610d2c5b7db661e93f5b3e4164dc7134ddea4d73d822f85","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10509.txt","text_sha256":"2d72b7098612c1a5d1dce60b40a9153703962d3fd4c1314fdcbb0c57e3599abb"}]},{"claim_sha256":"c6fd79e7a9735815ef3a604acd63f14fbeac6ea46275d6e311d7d4e98863ebad","current_canonical":false,"locations":[{"file_sha256":"484fbc search_rationale":"EO 10511 is a single-paragraph Railway Labor Act section 10 emergency board order. Its only commands are creation of the board, a thirty-day report deadline and a recited statutory standstill. Under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 (report deadline, option C) none of those ends the order; the first held end is the express revocation by EO 12553 on 1986-02-25 (signature date, no effective-date wording). The board's report date 1954-05-15 is a spent fact recorded in the clause reason. Savings: none found in EO 12553 or in the order itself. Searches: U.S. Code and eCFR number-citation searches found no hit for this order; the OFR disposition-note search lists only the EO 12553 revocation in the chain.\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Checked for an earlier end: (1) the order's own terms contain a report deadline and a post-report standstill but no sunset, so the report and standstill dates are spent facts and not ends under the report-deadline ruling; (2) no extension, amendment or revocation of EO 10511 other than EO 12553 appears in the dossier chain or in the number-citation scan of held texts; the report-time extensions described in the held report (where a report is held) were stipulations approved by the President, not held instruments, and affect only the deadline; (3) a general later rule is not treated as an end, only EO 12553, which names the order by number.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"The standstill is recited as provided by section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, so any no-change duty it describes arises under that statute and not only from the order; by the order's own terms it ran only to thirty days after the board's report, which was long before 1986. The order creates no continuing right, office or appropriation. No other command in the order survives the board's report.\",\"savings_check\":\"The text of EO 12553 contains no savings, exception, effective-date or notwithstanding wording (the held text was searched for those terms). The board's investigation, report and the standstill were spent in the 1950s, and the order created no accrued right or later-effective duty. The statute the order invokes is not affected by revoking the order.\",\"scope_check\":\"One clause covers the whole order; the order has no partial-scope command, no exception and no delayed effective date. The revocation in EO 12553 has no exception text, so it reaches the whole order.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[{"file_sha256":"e9c80d0c381d5045f956cd6a0abb24f9683079df4265dbb4c01a66e1e97cdc1a","path":"research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g05/packet/complete-v1/receipts/research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g05/packet/reviews/BLIND_REVIEW.jsonl","pointer":"line:6","review_kind":"independent_ai_source_review","scope":"whole_case","verdict":"upheld","version_binding":"exact"},{"file_sha256":"e9c80d0c381d5045f956cd6a0abb24f9683079df4265dbb4c01a66e1e97cdc1a","path":"research/excavator/claude-apollo-2026-10-31/g05/packet/reviews/BLIND_REVIEW.jsonl","pointer":"line:6","review_kind":"independent_ai_source_review","scope":"whole_case","verdict":"upheld","version_binding":"exact"}],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:10511#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:10511#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"fa58abf9d5cc4b75bad478246440611106b26f84fbfe528e6df3d931dfa716c9","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10511.txt","sha256":"fa58abf9d5cc4b75bad478246440611106b26f84fbfe528e6df3d931dfa716c9","state":"match"},"sha256":"42b9f8aeb2ded008bfce8808c17d5bd91b6cd24a0eaa65462b575b154f6356ff","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10511.txt","text_sha256":"fa58abf9d5cc4b75bad478246440611106b26f84fbfe528e6df3d931dfa716c9"}]},{"claim_sha256":"62b673e02656899fc0e22b7fb0a1da6e1e2af4b45316232f0546336f3f71f587","current_canonical":false,"locations":[{"file_sha256":"484fbc Inventory corrected: two clauses, offsets 3901 to 4520 and 11882 to 12075 of the held text. The text layer interleaves the order with unrelated Federal Register page text (a contents list) between its first and second parts, so the order's operative text is the first part (the enacting words) and the continuation, which are contiguous in the printed order. The dossier's span includes the interleaved unrelated page text. The order's heading and recitals precede the first part and contain no command. Text: The order recites that a dispute exists between the Pennsylvania Railroad, a carrier, and certain of its employees represented by the Transport Workers Union of America, C.I.O., Railroad Division, a labor organization. Acting under section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, as amended (45 U.S.C. 160), it creates a board of three members, to be appointed by the President, to investigate the dispute; it bars any board member from being pecuniarily or otherwise interested in any organization of railway employees or any carrier; it directs the board to report its findings to the President within thirty days from the date of the order; and, as provided by section 10, it bars the Pennsylvania Railroad or its employees from changing, except by agreement, the conditions out of which the dispute arose, from the date of the order until thirty days after the board has made its report to the President. Ending: Executive Order 12553 of February 25, 1986 (51 FR 7237, held govinfo text layer) has an enacting sentence reading, in part, \"it is hereby ordered that the following Executive Orders, and\" and, on the next line, \"any amendments thereto, are revoked:\" (the number 10635 appears in the table's number column, and the aligned subject entry in the separately extracted subject column (matched by position) reads as creating an emergency board to investigate a railroad labor dispute). The enacting sentence has no effective-date wording and no exception or saving clause, so the first end is dated on signing, 1986-02-25 (a reading disclosed for the reviewer; the March 3, 1986 printing date is not used). Report: the held National Mediation Board copy of the board's report (jobs/source-ladder/output/texts/queue_EO_10635_Emergency_Board_report_to_the_President_Pennsylvania_Railroad_and_Transport_Workers_U_4282a7bfa7.txt, a held text lead and not an authenticated original) has a cover page dated October 26, 1955; the board states that by stipulation of the parties the time for making the report was extended to October 31, 1955. The report deadline and the section 10 standstill are spent facts and not ends under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 (option C): taking October 26, 1955 as the date the board made its report, the order was functionally spent on October 26, 1955 and the thirty-day period after the report ran to November 25, 1955 (a computed date, thirty days after that cover or transmittal date, not a date shown in a source); the order stayed on the books until the express revocation. The dossier has no citable record covering the report's date line, so the report date is stated here and is not entered as a later event. Prior research: the packet reports no prior claim or review for this order. Searches: current-law:usc, current-law:ecfr and later-events:ofr_catalog, each marked complete in the packet with no coverage warning; they are the packet's machine searches.\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"Earlier-end check: the order's own terms contain a thirty-day report deadline and a standstill running until thirty days after the report, neither of which is an end; no stated fixed end date or termination clause appears in the held text. The packet's bounded number search of 33,118 held texts (clause drafts excluded) found citations only in this order's own printing, the National Mediation Board report copy. The dossier's chain of later events names only Executive Order 12553 as acting on this order, and the three selected machine searches show no revoking, superseding or terminating instrument before 1986-02-25.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"Independent commands: the creation of the board, the bar on interested members, the report deadline, the standstill all concern the single dispute the order addressed; none is a separate delegation, standing rule or continuing regulation. The standstill sentence restates section 10 of the Railway Labor Act, which continues on its own statutory force; the period it measures here was set by this order's creation of the board and nothing held shows it still running.\",\"savings_check\":\"Savings: Executive Order 12553 contains no saving clause (the enacting sentence is followed only by the table). The order's one-time commands (creating the board, the report, the status quo period) were exhausted by performance or lapse, section 10 of the Railway Labor Act continues independently and needs no support from this order, and no surviving appointment, transfer or right is shown in any held source. State none_after_bounded_search.\",\"scope_check\":\"Scope: Executive Order 12553 revokes the order by number, the table entry matches the order's subject, and the enacting sentence carries no exception, so the revocation reaches the whole order and every clause inventoried here.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:10635#a"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:10635#a"},{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:10635#b"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:10635#b"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"f5bd696fe82dbf86823b2927593bea35d43b2bcb7dc9eb3b4c851982f8fd3252","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10635.txt","sha256":"f5bd696fe82dbf86823b2927593bea35d43b2bcb7dc9eb3b4c851982f8fd3252","state":"match"},"sha256":"10310a3640c4f7516b3d5db32207291bae0ba608940892cbe16e8c4ce470b460","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10635.txt","text_sha256":"f5bd696fe82dbf86823b2927593bea35d43b2bcb7dc9eb3b4c851982f8fd3252"}]}]}

I read the whole order; the representation is the machine-extracted text layer of the official PDF (OCR for this period), and I did not inspect the page images. End: express revocation by Executive Order 12553 on 1986-02-25, under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 on report deadlines (option C): a report deadline and a spent standstill are not ends. Searches: current-law:usc, current-law:ecfr and later-events:ofr_catalog, each marked complete in the dossier; the U.S. Code and eCFR searches found no hit naming this order. The held-text number search for this order found no held later order that cites it other than Executive Order 12553.\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"The order contains no fixed end date and no termination clause. Its commands are a bar on interested board members, a requirement that the board report within thirty days from the date of the order (due by May 22, 1960), and a standstill running from the date of the order until thirty days after the report. The report deadline and the standstill are spent facts, not ends, under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 on report deadlines, and neither is claimed as the end. A held lead copy of the report of Emergency Board No. 130 (a text of the front matter of the National Mediation Board publication, graded held_text_lead, a grade the claim format does not accept as a cited source) is dated June 8, 1960; the report's table of contents lists a letter of extension of the time limit. Thirty days after June 8, 1960 is July 8, 1960. Those dates come from a lead and are disclosed, not claimed as events or as the end: the clause is described only as functionally spent on June 8, 1960, the report date, with the standstill functionally spent on July 8, 1960. My reading of the held texts of Executive Order 12553 and the number search of the held texts show no earlier revoking, superseding or terminating instrument for this order.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"Each command (create the board, bar interested members, require the report, freeze conditions) depends on this order and on the dispute it created; none is a separate delegation, continuing regulation or standing rule. The standstill sentence applies section 10 of the Railway Labor Act (45 U.S.C. 160); the statute is a separate source of law that an Executive order does not revoke, but the periods measured here were set by this order's creation of the board, and no held claim-grade source shows them still running when the order was revoked.\",\"savings_check\":\"Executive Order 12553 was read in full: it consists of one revoking sentence, a table of the revoked orders by number and subject, an editorial note and the signature block, and it contains no saving or transition provision. No residual effect of this order is shown to depend on its continued force. Section 10 of the Railway Labor Act continues independently as stated in the independent-command check.\",\"scope_check\":\"One clause is inventoried: the operative text from the order heading through the standstill sentence (the recitals, the creation of the board of three members, the bar on interested members, the report requirement and the standstill). The signature, the date line and any attached lists follow it; the attached lists, where present, only identify carriers and labor organizations and contain no command. Executive Order 12553 revokes the order by number as a whole, not in part, so every command in the clause ends on the same date. The ending source cited is the revoking sentence and table of Executive Order 12553.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:10875#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:10875#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"356ec91ef4035dca6b8a87e1e491ca18486730728f21ce3c03835b6e37c87ff8","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10875.txt","sha256":"356ec91ef4035dca6b8a87e1e491ca18486730728f21ce3c03835b6e37c87ff8","state":"match"},"sha256":"6f82000d5a91dd4531a03d5ce406ff62f2fd9a8657f743a331e9200928ca6d23","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10875.txt","text_sha256":"356ec91ef4035dca6b8a87e1e491ca18486730728f21ce3c03835b6e37c87ff8"}]}]}

I read the whole order; the representation is the machine-extracted text layer of the official PDF (OCR for this period), and I did not inspect the page images. End: express revocation by Executive Order 12553 on 1986-02-25, under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 on report deadlines (option C): a report deadline and a spent standstill are not ends. Searches: current-law:usc, current-law:ecfr and later-events:ofr_catalog, each marked complete in the dossier; the U.S. Code and eCFR searches found no hit naming this order. The held-text number search for this order found no held later order that cites it other than Executive Order 12553.\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"The order contains no fixed end date and no termination clause. Its commands are a bar on interested board members, a requirement that the board report within thirty days from the date of the order (due by October 28, 1960), and a standstill running from the date of the order until thirty days after the report. The report deadline and the standstill are spent facts, not ends, under the owner ruling of 2026-10-21 on report deadlines, and neither is claimed as the end. A held lead copy of the report of Emergency Board No. 133 (a text of the front matter of the National Mediation Board publication, graded held_text_lead, a grade the claim format does not accept as a cited source) is dated December 10, 1960; the report states that the time for filing it was extended to December 10, 1960 by the President on the consent of the parties. Thirty days after December 10, 1960 is January 9, 1961. Those dates come from a lead and are disclosed, not claimed as events or as the end: the clause is described only as functionally spent on December 10, 1960, the report date, with the standstill functionally spent on January 9, 1961. My reading of the held texts of Executive Order 12553 and the number search of the held texts show no earlier revoking, superseding or terminating instrument for this order.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"Each command (create the board, bar interested members, require the report, freeze conditions) depends on this order and on the dispute it created; none is a separate delegation, continuing regulation or standing rule. The standstill sentence applies section 10 of the Railway Labor Act (45 U.S.C. 160); the statute is a separate source of law that an Executive order does not revoke, but the periods measured here were set by this order's creation of the board, and no held claim-grade source shows them still running when the order was revoked.\",\"savings_check\":\"Executive Order 12553 was read in full: it consists of one revoking sentence, a table of the revoked orders by number and subject, an editorial note and the signature block, and it contains no saving or transition provision. No residual effect of this order is shown to depend on its continued force. Section 10 of the Railway Labor Act continues independently as stated in the independent-command check.\",\"scope_check\":\"One clause is inventoried: the operative text from the order heading through the standstill sentence (the recitals, the creation of the board of three members, the bar on interested members, the report requirement and the standstill). The signature, the date line and any attached lists follow it; the attached lists, where present, only identify carriers and labor organizations and contain no command. Executive Order 12553 revokes the order by number as a whole, not in part, so every command in the clause ends on the same date. The ending source cited is the revoking sentence and table of Executive Order 12553.\"}","review_dependency_issues":[],"reviews":[],"scopes":[{"aligned_current_scopes":["EO:10888#whole"],"alignment":"exact_quote_and_source","different_current_fields":[],"scope_id":"EO:10888#whole"}],"source_pins":[{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761","state":"match"},"sha256":"945348afbba863ae7dc1fbe7df8759682fe1733962734a9255a1062c0561048c","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_12553.txt","text_sha256":"6361bdc557b6acaeae6e6908cded18f4f8fef9fde1a97cc11fb7c12c8cdea761"},{"held_text":{"expected_sha256":"c50c0dcf91946c4cd6aeb053f2538e8973e0862a2760bfa3a3a97b66f36a0862","path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10888.txt","sha256":"c50c0dcf91946c4cd6aeb053f2538e8973e0862a2760bfa3a3a97b66f36a0862","state":"match"},"sha256":"c33fddf85b904286a4759b60995534ce55c1bf4b1e915b7d62ee0fb1d33c69b1","text_path":"jobs/fr-originals/data/full_run/texts/EO_10888.txt","text_sha256":"c50c0dcf91946c4cd6aeb053f2538e8973e0862a2760bfa3a3a97b66f36a0862"}]},{"claim_sha256":"e9d1164aa31bc8635be4734a0b3dbfd812bae2ccb489c136a53c75c539e88fa9","current_canonical":false,"locations":[{"file_sha256":"484fbc0cef24f803144e256c096ce03d5df24dd0b311aa771dff2857257712da","path":"research/excavator/claude-baldus2-2026-11-25/m02/packet-v2-sup/applied-v1/CLAIMS.proposed.jsonl","pointer":"line:5"}],"reported_material_gaps":[],"research_rationale":"Replacement claim for EO:10888 applying owner rule D-20261108-board-rule to the filed claim's open step. Original: the dossier's held original ORIGINAL:EO:10888, the same printing the filed claim cites. Report: NMB report of Emergency Board No. 133, held bytes jobs/ptolemy-custody/output/raw/ (SHA-256 f099d44d2ca4...), text jobs/source-ladder/output/texts; the cited span runs from the cover through its date line and board-number line. Report dated 1960-12-10. Clause ends: board 1960-12-10 (completion of its task); no-change period 1961-01-09 (inclusive; exclusive 1961-01-08). Whole-order end 1961-01-09. Formal disposition EO 12553 of 1986-02-25. The filed claim's first end, EO 12553, is kept as the formal disposition under the 2026-10-28 ruling (an endpoint needs a reason) and not as the first end.\nMember checks: {\"earlier_end_check\":\"No earlier end: the board's task could not be complete before its report of 1960-12-10, and the no-change period runs from the report. A search of the held corpus for this order's number found it only in its own printing, the Federal Register and codification tables ('Revoked by: EO 12553, February 25, 1986') and the NMB report front matter; no earlier revoking, superseding or amending instrument was found, and the OFR catalog search shows none.\",\"independent_command_check\":\"No command other than create-investigate-report and the no-change sentence is present in the held order text (read in full).\",\"savings_check\":\"Savings state none_after_bounded_search: EO 12553's held text has no savings wording reaching EO:10888; the board's work and any agreement reached are completed past events.\",\"scope_check\":\"The two clauses cover the order's operative text: the board clause and the no-change sentence; the recitals and heading stay with the board clause. EO 12553 revokes the whole order by number and reaches any amendments; it is a later event of both ## Valla review leads and component attestations

Review lead only. Every case needs separate applicability, earlier-ending, scope, independent-command, savings and independent whole-case review.
Preparation attestations never approve legal conclusions or replace whole-case review.
